## Page 7

**Arabic (original):**
الجزء الأول
من كتاب
شمس المعارف
الكبرى
ولطائف العوارف
للشيخ أحمد بن علي البوني قدس الله روحه المتوفى سنة ٦٢٢ اثنين وعشرين وستمائة
قال في كشف الظنون والمقصود من هذا الكتاب أن يعلم بذلك شرف أسماء الله تعالى وما أودع
في بحرها من أنواع الجواهر الحكميات وكيف التصريف بالأسماء والدعوات وما تابعها
من حروف السور والآيات ليتصل بها إلى الحضرة الربانية من غير تعب ويتصل بها إلى
رغائب الدنيا بلا نصب انتهى
❖ ويليه ❖
رسالة ميزان العدل في مقاصد أحكام الرمل ❖ ورسالة فواتح الرغائب في خصوصات
أوقات الكواكب ❖ ورسالة زهر المروج في دلائل البروج ❖ ورسالة لطائف الإشارة
في خصائص الكواكب السيارة ❖ تأليف العلامة الفاضل السيد عبد القادر الحسيني الأدهمي
❖ نفع الله بعلومه المسلمين آمين
❖ تنبيه ❖
حيث إن كتاب شمس المعارف لشدة احتياج الناس إليه طبع مراراً (بمصر والهند) وقد
اعتنت المطابع المصرية بتصحيحه لكنه لم يخل من غلط وتحريف لعدم وجود نسخ صحيحة
وقد طبع هذه المرة مقابلاً على نسخ « مصر والهند » ونسخة أخرى بخط الحاج مرزا حسين
مع الدقة في التصحيح بمعرفة لجنة من أفاضل علماء مصر وأعاد تصحيحها الأستاذ الكبير الشيخ
عبد الرحمن الجزيري فجاءت بحمد الله هذه النسخة من أجل النسخ طبعاً وتصحيحاً
طبع بمطبعة
مصطفى البابي الحلبي وأولاده بمصر

**English:**
The first part
from the book
Shams al-Ma‘arif
al-Kubra
and Lata’if al-‘Awarif
by the Shaykh Ahmad ibn Ali al-Buni, may Allah sanctify his spirit, deceased in the year 622, twenty-two and six hundred
He said in Kashf al-Zunun: and the intended purpose of this book is that by it one may know the nobility of the Names of Allah تعالى and what has been deposited
in their sea of kinds of the wisdom-jewels, and how the disposition by the names and the invocations, and what follows them
of the letters of the surahs and the verses, so that by them one may connect to the Divine Presence without toil and connect by them to
the desires of the world without trouble. انتهى
❖ And following it ❖
Risalat Mizan al-‘Adl fi Maqasid Ahkam al-Raml ❖ and Risalat Fawatih al-Ragha’ib fi Khususat
Awtat al-Kawakib ❖ and Risalat Zahr al-Muruj fi Dala’il al-Buruj ❖ and Risalat Lata’if al-Isharah
fi Khasa’is al-Kawakib al-Sayyarah ❖ authored by the learned scholar, the noble السيد عبد القادر الحسيني الأدهمي
❖ May Allah benefit the Muslims by his sciences, amen
❖ Notice ❖
Since the book Shams al-Ma‘arif, because of the شدت need of people for it, has been printed many times (in Egypt and India), and the Egyptian presses have taken care in correcting it, yet it has not been free from error and distortion due to the absence of correct copies, and it has been printed this time collated against copies of “Egypt and India” and another copy in the handwriting of Hajj Mirza Husayn,
with precision in correction by the knowledge of a committee from the eminent scholars of Egypt, and it was corrected again by the great professor Shaykh
Abd al-Rahman al-Jaziri, so this copy has come, praise be to Allah, as the most excellent of copies in printing and correction
Printed at the press of
Mustafa al-Babi al-Halabi and his sons in Egypt

## Page 8

**Arabic (original):**
وَلِلَّهِ الْأَسْمَاءُ الْحُسْنَىٰ فَادْعُوهُ بِهَا

بِسْمِ اللَّهِ الرَّحْمَٰنِ الرَّحِيمِ

شَهَادَةٌ أَزَلٌ فَمِنْ نُورِ هَذِهِ الشَّهَادَةِ اغْتَرَفَ الْمُصْطَفَوْنَ عِلْمًا فَافْهَمْ ذَلِكَ وَالتَّرْتِيبَ الَّذِي بَدَا فِي الشَّهَادَتَيْنِ
الْمُتَّصِلَتَيْنِ بِالْمَلَائِكَةِ الْكِرَامِ وَأُولِي الْعِلْمِ فَهَذِهِ شَهَادَةُ الْأَبَدِ فَإِنَّ فِيهِمْ سِرَّ هَاتَيْنِ الشَّهَادَتَيْنِ شَاهِدُ
الْمَلَكُوتَيْنِ وَمَا أَوْدَعَاهُ بِسِرِّ الِاتِّصَالِ بِالْكَشْفِيَّاتِ وَلِكُلِّ هَيْئَةٍ تَوَصُّلُهُ إِلَىٰ جَمِيعِ الْحِكْمِيَّاتِ
وَأَسْأَلُ اللَّهَ الْحَيَّ الْقَيُّومَ أَنْ يَجْعَلَهُ خَالِصًا صَدَقَةً مَقْبُولَةً بَيْنَ يَدَيَّ نَجْوَايَ وَأَنْ يَصْحَبَنِي رُوحُ ارْتِيَاحِهِ
فِي تَقَلُّبِي وَمَثْوَايَ وَأَنْ يُوضِحَ لِي وَلَكُمُ الطَّرِيقَ وَيَمُنَّ عَلَيْنَا وَإِيَّاكُمْ بِأَنْوَارِ التَّحْقِيقِ إِنَّ هَذِهِ الْبَرْقَةَ
الرَّحْمُوتِيَّةَ وَالشُّمُوسَ الْمُشْرِقَةَ الْمُضِيئَةَ سُبُلَ الْعَارِفِينَ وَمِنْهَاجَ الصِّدِّيقِينَ وَمَهْيَعَةَ الصَّالِحِينَ
لِحَضْرَةِ قُدْسِ رَبِّ الْعَالَمِينَ رَبِّ الْأَرْبَابِ وَفَاتِقِ الْأَسْبَابِ وَرَافِعِ الْحِجَابِ الْمُخْتَرَعِ بِلَا مِثَالٍ
الْمُنَزَّهِ عَنِ الْأَشْكَالِ الدَّائِمِ الَّذِي لَمْ يَزَلْ مَنْعُوتًا بِنُعُوتِ الْجَمَالِ دَائِمِ الْوُجُودِ فِي الْأَزَلِ رَافِعِ
الْعُلْوِيَّاتِ بِتَقْدِيرِهِ وَحِكْمَتِهِ وَبَاسِطِ السُّفْلِيَّاتِ بِقُدْرَتِهِ وَإِرَادَتِهِ لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا هُوَ الْكَبِيرُ الْمُتَعَالِ
الْمُحْتَجِبِ بِحُجُبِ الْأَنْوَارِ الْمُسْتَتِرِ عَنْ سَائِرِ الْأَسْرَارِ الْخَفِيِّ عَنْ سُرَادِقَاتِ الْأَبْصَارِ وَهُوَ يُدْرِكُ
الْأَبْصَارَ بِبَطْنِهِ بِذَاتِهِ فِي أَزَلِيَّتِهِ وَظَهَرَ بِصِفَاتِهِ فِي أَبَدِيَّتِهِ وَاسْتَعْلَنَ بِآلِهِ فِي سَرْمَدِيَّتِهِ وَتَجَلَّىٰ
بِأَفْعَالِهِ فِي أَبَدِيَّتِهِ هُوَ الْأَوَّلُ فِي الْأَزَلِ وَالْآخِرُ فِي الْأَبَدِ وَالظَّاهِرُ فِي السَّرْمَدِ جَلَّ عَنِ
الْجَوَاهِرِ وَالْأَعْرَاضِ وَعَنِ الْأَجْرَامِ وَالْأَبْعَاضِ وَعَنِ التَّصَرُّفِ بِالْأَغْرَاضِ لَا تَحْوِيهِ
الْجِهَاتُ وَالْأَقْطَارُ وَلَا يَلِيهِ تَعَاقُبُ الْأَدْوَارِ وَلَا يَفْنِيهِ مُرُورُ اللَّيْلِ وَالنَّهَارِ أَحْمَدُهُ
سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَىٰ وَكُلُّ شَيْءٍ عِنْدَهُ بِمِقْدَارٍ عَالِمِ الْغَيْبِ وَالشَّهَادَةِ الْكَبِيرُ الْمُتَعَالِ وَأَشْهَدُ أَنْ لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا
اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لَا شَرِيكَ لَهُ شَهَادَةً تَصْحَبُ الْأَرْوَاحَ بِالتَّثْبِيتِ فِي الْبَرْزَخِيَّاتِ وَأَشْهَدُ لَكُمْ أَحْصَاهُ خَلِيقَتُهُ
أَحْيَاءً وَأَمْوَاتًا وَقَدْ رَأَى الْأَمْوَاتَ وَالْأَقْوَاتَ الْعَالَمَ بِمَا مَضَىٰ وَمَا هُوَ آتٍ وَمُحْيِي الْأَمْوَاتِ
بَعْدَ مَا كَانَتْ رُفَاتًا وَأَشْهَدُ أَنَّ مُحَمَّدًا عَبْدُهُ وَرَسُولُهُ شَمْسُ الْمِلَّةِ وَمُنْقِذُ الْعِبَادِ مِنَ الشِّرْكِ
وَالذِّلَّةِ

16053

**English:**
And to Allah belong the Most Beautiful Names, so call upon Him by them.

In the name of Allah, the Merciful, the Compassionate.

A testimony, pre-eternity; and from the light of this testimony the chosen ones drew up knowledge, so understand that, and the arrangement which appeared in the two testimonies
connected with the noble angels and those possessed of knowledge. So this is the testimony of eternity, for in them is the secret of these two testimonies, the witness
of the two kingdoms, and what He deposited in it by the secret of connection with the unveilings, and for every form its reaching to all the wisdoms.
And I ask Allah, the Living, the Self-Subsisting, that He make it a pure, accepted alms before my whispered prayer, and that the spirit of its ease accompany me
in my turning about and my abode, and that He make clear for me and for you the path, and bestow upon us and upon you the lights of verification. Verily this lightning-flash,
the merciful, and the shining, illuminating suns, are the paths of the knowers, and the way of the veracious, and the broad road of the righteous,
for the presence of the holiness of the Lord of the worlds, the Lord of the lords, and the splitter of the causes, and the lifter of the veil, invented without example,
the transcendent above forms, the everlasting, who has not ceased to be described with the attributes of beauty, ever-existent in pre-eternity, the lifter
of the higher things by His determination and His wisdom, and the spreader of the lower things by His power and His will; there is no god except Him, the Great, the Most High,
the veiled by the veils of lights, hidden from all the secrets, concealed from the pavilions of the sights, and He perceives
the sights by His inwardness, by His Essence, in His pre-eternity, and He appeared by His attributes in His eternity, and He became manifest by His instruments in His sempiternity, and He manifested Himself
by His acts in His eternity. He is the First in pre-eternity, and the Last in eternity, and the Manifest in sempiternity. Exalted is He above
substances and accidents, and above bodies and parts, and above being disposed of by purposes. The directions and the regions do not contain Him,
and the succession of the cycles does not follow Him, and the passing of night and day does not annihilate Him. I praise Him,
glorified is He and exalted, and everything with Him is by measure, Knower of the unseen and the witnessed, the Great, the Most High. And I bear witness that there is no god except
Allah, alone, no partner has He, a testimony that accompanies the spirits with تثبيت in the barzakh-realms. And I bear witness for you: His creation has counted it,
the living and the dead, and He has seen the dead and the powers, the world with what has passed and what is coming, and the Reviver of the dead
after they had been bones. And I bear witness that Muhammad is His servant and His Messenger, the sun of the creed and the deliverer of the servants from shirk
and abasement.

## Page 9

**Arabic (original):**
— ٣ —

والدالّ الذي دار فلك التوحيد بدعوته ❊ واستنارت شموس حكمته ❊ وغارت أنجم الضلالة برؤيته ❊ وأسفر صبح التوحيد بسعادته ❊ صلى الله عليه وعلى آله أفضل الصلوات ❊ ورضي الله عن الصحابة المحققين الصديقين ❊ رضي الله عنهم أجمعين ❊ صلاة تبلغهم أعلى المراتب وأرفع الدرجات ❊ (أما بعد) ❊ فالحق أعلام وللحقيقة نظام وللأرواح بالمعارف الإلهيات إلمام والوسيلة مطلوبة والقدرة على أقسامها موهوبة والسعادة بشموس الكمال مقرونة والخبرة الأبدية باستعمال مناسك الشريعة موهوبة وأعلى الدرجات في عليين درجة العاملين وأعلاها منزلة الهادين المحققين ولا منزلة لعالم في دين الله لا يفيد كما أنه لا وجود حياة لحقيقة نفس لا تفيد وإن أبعد الناس من السعادة من استهان بأحكام الملة وأخل بشروط المحققين من أهل القبلة (وإني) لما رأيت كلام الأجلاء ممن علت كلمتهم وانبسطت في الآفاق حكمتهم وعمت في البرايا بركتهم قد ألفوا في التصريف بالأسماء والصفات وأسرار الحروف والأذكار والدعوات وقد رغب إليّ من تعلق بي وده في توضيح ما ألفوه وذخيرة ما كنزوه فأجبته مع الإقرار بالعجز عن فهم مدارك السلف الماضين والأئمة المحققين الهادين ورجوت من الله بذل الاعتراف والاقتراف أن يمدني من أرواح أرواحهم بلطائف الإسعاف فيكون النطق موافقاً للتحقيق ومفصلاً بلسان التصديق (فأقول) ❊ بالله المستعان وعليه التكلان إن المقصود من فصول هذا الكتاب العلم بشرف أسماء الله تعالى وما أودع الله تعالى في بحرها من أنواع الجواهر الحكميات واللطائف الإلهيات وكيف التصرف بأسماء الدعوات وما تابعها من حروف السور والآيات وجعلت هذا الكتاب فصولاً ليدل كل فصل على ما اختاره وأحصاه من علوم دقيقة يتوصل بها للحضرة الربانية من غير تعب ولا إدراك مشقة وما يتوصل منها إلى رغائب الدنيا وما يرغب فيها وسميت هذا الكتاب المنتخب العديم المثل الرفيع العلم (بشمس المعارف ولطائف العوارف) لما في ضمنه من لطائف التصرف وعوارف التأثيرات فحرام على من وقع كتابي هذا في يده أن يبديه لغير أهله أو يبوح به في غير محله فإنه مهما فعل ذلك أحرمه الله تعالى منافعه ومنعت عنه فوائده وبركته ولا تمسه إلا وأنت طاهر ولا تقرأ به إلا إذا كنت ذاكراً لتفوز منه بما تريد ولا تصرفه إلا فيما يرضى الله تعالى فإنه كتاب الأولياء والصالحين والطائعين والمريدين والعاملين الراغبين فكن به ضنيناً ولا تدع منه قليلاً ولا كثيراً وليكن يقينك صادقاً وإيمانك بحقائقه واثقاً إنما الأعمال بالنيات وإنما لكل امرئ ما نوى وإذا قامت لك نية في عمل من الأعمال فتؤمن به وتصدق لقوله عليه الصلاة والسلام لا يدعو أحدكم إلا وهو موقن بالإجابة واقطع على عملك بالصحة لقوله عليه الصلاة والسلام إذا سأل أحدكم فليعزم المسألة فإنه لا يكره ويقين الإجابة واقطع على عملك بالصحة لقوله صلى الله عليه وسلم يستجاب لأحدكم إذا لم يعجل فيقول دعوت فلم يستجب لي ❊ وإياك أن تستبطئ الإجابة ولا تزل متطهراً دائماً ومنتظرها ❊ ويشتمل هذا القانون القويم والطريق المستقيم على أربعين فصلاً كل فصل يشتمل على معان وإشارات ورموز خفيات وظاهرات فتدبره بعقلك وتأمله بفكرك ❊ وهذه الفصول (الفصل الأول) في الحروف

**English:**
— 3 —

And the one who, by his call, made the sphere of tawhid revolve ❊ and the suns of his wisdom shone forth ❊ and the stars of misguidance sank away by his sight ❊ and the dawn of tawhid broke forth by his felicity ❊ may the best of prayers be upon him and upon his family ❊ and may Allah be pleased with the verifying, truthful Companions ❊ may Allah be pleased with them all together ❊ a prayer that reaches them the highest ranks and the loftiest degrees ❊ (Now then) ❊ then the truth has banners and for the reality there is a system and for the spirits, by the divine knowledges, an acquaintance, and the means is sought and the ability to its divisions is bestowed, and felicity is coupled with the suns of perfection, and eternal experience by the use of the rites of the Shari‘ah is bestowed, and the highest degrees in ‘Illiyyin are the degree of the doers, and the highest of them is the station of the guiding, verifying ones, and there is no station for a scholar in the religion of Allah who does not benefit, just as there is no existence of life for the reality of a soul that does not benefit, and the farthest of people from felicity is he who makes light of the rulings of the millah and breaches the conditions of the verifying ones from among the people of the qiblah (And I), when I saw the speech of the eminent ones, those whose word has become lofty and whose wisdom has spread in the horizons and whose blessing has encompassed the creatures, they had authored in the conjugation of names and attributes and the secrets of letters and remembrances and supplications, and there had inclined to me from one attached to me his affection in clarifying what they had authored and the treasure of what they had stored up, so I answered him, with acknowledgment of incapacity to understand the perceptions of the past forefathers and the guiding verifying imams, and I hoped from Allah, by the exertion of acknowledgment and acquisition, that He would extend to me from the spirits of their spirits with subtle aids, so that the utterance would be in accordance with verification and detailed with the tongue of confirmation (so I say) ❊ in Allah is the sought help and upon Him is the reliance: the intended purpose of the chapters of this book is knowledge of the nobility of the names of Allah تعالى and what Allah تعالى has deposited in their sea of the kinds of الحكميات jewels and divine لطائف, and how to act with the names of the supplications and what followed them of the letters of the surahs and the verses, and I made this book chapters so that every chapter would indicate what I selected and counted of subtle sciences by which one reaches the divine presence without fatigue and without perceiving hardship, and what by them one reaches the desires of the world and what is desired in it, and I named this selected, peerless, lofty-knowledge book (Shams al-Ma‘arif wa Lata’if al-‘Awarif) because of what is within it of subtle acts of disposition and the knowledges of influences. So it is forbidden upon whoever this book of mine falls into his hand that he display it to other than its people or divulge it in other than its place, for whenever he does that Allah تعالى will deprive him of its benefits and its profits and its blessing will be withheld from him, and do not touch it except while you are pure, and do not read it except if you are remembering, so that you may obtain from it what you want, and do not direct it except in what Allah تعالى is pleased with, for it is the book of the awliya’, and the righteous, and the obedient, and the seekers, and the doers, the desirous ones, so be stingy with it and do not leave from it a little nor a lot, and let your certainty be truthful and your faith in its realities confident. Actions are only by intentions, and only for every man is what he intended, and if an intention arises for you in an act among the acts, then believe in it and affirm, according to His saying عليه الصلاة والسلام: let none of you supplicate except while he is certain of the response, and decide upon your act with correctness, according to His saying عليه الصلاة والسلام: if one of you asks, then let him resolve the request, for it is not disliked, and the certainty of response, and decide upon your act with correctness, according to His saying صلى الله عليه وسلم: one of you is answered if he does not hasten, saying, “I supplicated, but I was not answered.” ❊ And beware that you do not deem the response slow, and do not cease being purified always and awaiting it ❊ And this upright law and straight path comprises forty chapters; every chapter comprises meanings and indications and hidden and manifest symbols, so ponder it with your intellect and contemplate it with your thought ❊ And these chapters (the first chapter) are in the letters

The muʿjama and what is arranged in it of the secrets and the concealments. ﴿ Chapter Two ﴾ in breaking and expansion and the arrangement of
works in the times and hours. ﴿ Chapter Three ﴾ in the rulings of the twenty-eight mansions of the moon,
the celestial. ﴿ Chapter Four ﴾ in the rulings of the twelve signs of the zodiac and what there is for them of the indications and the connections.
﴿ Chapter Five ﴾ in the secrets of the basmala and what there is for it of the hidden properties and blessings. ﴿ Chapter Six ﴾
in seclusion and the masters of retreat leading to the upper realms. ﴿ Chapter Seven ﴾ in the names by which Jesus
upon him السلام used to bring the dead to life. ﴿ Chapter Eight ﴾ in the four tawqīfāt and what there is for them of the chapters
and the circles. ﴿ Chapter Nine ﴾ in the properties of the beginnings of the Qur’an and the clear verses. ﴿ Chapter Ten ﴾
in the secrets of al-Fātiḥa and its supplications and its famous properties. ﴿ Chapter Eleven ﴾ in the inventions
and in the merciful lights. ﴿ Chapter Twelve ﴾ in the Greatest Name of Allah and what there is for it of the hidden dispositions.
﴿ Chapter Thirteen ﴾ in the fallen parts of al-Fātiḥa and what there is for them of the wafqs and the supplications. ﴿ Chapter
Fourteen ﴾ in the spiritual exercises and the remembrances and the answered supplications, the subjugated ones. ﴿ Chapter Fifteen ﴾
in the conditions necessary for some, not for some, in the beginnings to the suns of the endings. ﴿ Chapter Sixteen ﴾ in the Beautiful Names of Allah and their times, the beneficial tried ones. ﴿ Chapter Seventeen ﴾ in
the properties of Kهيعص and its letters, the lordly, the most holy. ﴿ Chapter Eighteen ﴾ in the properties of the Verse
of the Throne and what is in it of the hidden blessings. ﴿ Chapter Nineteen ﴾ in the properties of some wafqs
and the beneficial talismans. ﴿ Chapter Twenty ﴾ in Sūrat Yā Sīn and what there is for it of the answered supplications.
﴿ Chapter Twenty-One ﴾ in the Beautiful Names of Allah and their patterns and what there is for every pattern of the supplications and the dispositions.
﴿ Chapter Twenty-Two ﴾ in the second pattern and what is in it of the gift-names. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Three ﴾
in the third pattern and what indicates the adyāt attributes. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Four ﴾ in the fourth pattern and what
is in it of the secrets of the Lord of the creatures. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Five ﴾ in the fifth pattern and what is in it of the selected
properties. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Six ﴾ in the sixth pattern and what is in it of the secrets of the necessitating فرضيات.
﴿ Chapter Twenty-Seven ﴾ in the seventh pattern from the Names of Allah تعالى and what there is for them of the blessings. ﴿ Chapter
Twenty-Eight ﴾ in the eighth pattern from the Beautiful Names and their hidden secrets. ﴿ Chapter Twenty-Nine ﴾ in the ninth
pattern from the Beautiful Names and what there is for them of the dispositions. ﴿ Chapter Thirty ﴾ in the
tenth pattern from the Beautiful Names and their beneficial secrets. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-One ﴾ in the letters
of Arabic and what there is for them of the planets and the servants and the minerals and the seclusions. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Two ﴾ in the secrets
of uncovering the معنويات of ʿarūḍ. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Three ﴾ in the explanation of the secrets of the Circle of Encompassment and what has appeared
from it of the foundations and the definitions. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Four ﴾ in the science of the Zāyirja and the proportions of the letters and the signs
of the zodiac and the famous balances. ﴿ Chapter Thirty-Five ﴾ in the lettered hidden thing by the Jafr rules. ﴿ Chapter
Thirty-Six ﴾ in the divine effusion and the radiant light and the honored stone and the properties of plants.

uses

**Arabic (original):**
٥

استخدامات الحروف وخلواتها على الجمل والتفصيلات (الفصل التاسع والثلاثون) في شرح أسماء الله
الحسنى كما وردت بطريق الإيضاح والتفصيلات (الفصل الأربعون) في الأدعية المفردة المدعو بها في
سائر الليالي والأوقات

(الفصل الأول في الحروف المعجمة وما فيها من الأسرار والإضمارات)

أقول وبالله التوفيق والهداية قد انقسمت مطالب الراغبين إلى قسمين دنيوي وأخروي وينقسم
كل واحد منهما إلى أقسام بحسب المقاصد وقد تكلم الناس في معارضة الأوقات والوقوف على الكواكب
والرياضات وأفعال الطلسمات قبل وضع هذا الكتاب والحديث عليه وهذا العلم متسع رغب فيه خلق
كثير وعليه لاسيما من وجد لذلك أثرا عظيما فأردت معارضة ذلك بوصف يجري مجرى الحاسة في أنحاء
أهل هذا العلم وتكلمت فيه الحكماء الأوائل ووافق على ذلك القول كثير من الناس فتلك إن كثرت
في الدنيا أضرت في الآخرة وهذا الذي أذكره لك تنفع به في الدنيا والآخرة
(فصل في ذكر الحروف المعجمة أهي أصول الكلام وأساسه وبها يرتفع بناؤه)
(واعلم) أن للأعداد أسرارا كما أن للحروف آثارا وأن العالم العلوي يمد العالم السفلي فعالم العرش يمد عالم
الكرسي وعالم الكرسي يمد فلك زحل وفلك زحل يمد فلك المشتري وفلك المشتري يمد فلك المريخ وفلك المريخ
يمد فلك الشمس وفلك الشمس يمد فلك الزهرة وفلك الزهرة يمد فلك عطارد وفلك عطارد يمد فلك القمر
وفلك القمر يمد فلك الحرارة وفلك الحرارة يمد فلك الرطوبة وفلك الرطوبة يمد فلك البرودة وفلك
البرودة يمد فلك اليبوسة وفلك اليبوسة يمد فلك الهواء وفلك الهواء يمد فلك الماء وفلك الماء يمد فلك
التراب وفلك التراب يمد فلك زحل في العلويات حرف الجيم والأعداد الواقعة عليها ثلاثة
على الجملة (وأما) على التفصيل فثلاثة وخمسون هكذا الميم بأربع والباء بثلاثة (وهو
أيضا) بثلاثة أحرف وله من السفليات حرف الصاد وهو في العدد تسعون وتلك في العلويات على الجملة خمسة
وهو حرف الهاء وله من الأوفاق المخمس وفلك المشتري ستة وهو حرف الواو وله من الأوفاق المسدس
(وأما) تصريف فلك الزهرة فله حرف الزاي وله من الأوفاق المسبع وأما تصريف فلك عطارد فله
من العدد ثمانية وهو حرف الحاء وله من الأوفاق المثمن (وأما) تصريف فلك القمر فله من العدد
تسعة وهو حرف الطاء وله من الأوفاق المتسع وزحل له المثلث المشهور بين العلماء فافهم ذلك
(فصل في نسبة الذات الإنسانية)
فالعرش له حرف الألف والكرسي له حرف الباء وزحل له حرف الجيم وهكذا إلى القمر كما تقدم
(فصل) والحروف على أنواع منها ما يبدأ به على اليمين وهي حروف العرب ومنها ما يبدأ
به على الشمال وهي الرومية واليونانية والقبطية وكل كتابة على اليمين متصلة وكل كتابة على الشمال
منفصلة فافهم ذلك (والحروف) ثمانية وعشرون غير لام ألف وهي تمام التسعة والعشرين وذاك
عدد المنازل القمرية ولما كانت المنازل يظهر منها فوق الأرض أربعة عشر كانت هذه الحروف
منها ما يدغم مع لام التعريف وهي أربعة عشر حرفا ت ث د ذ ر ز س ش ص ض ط ظ ل ن

**English:**
5

Uses of the letters and their retreats upon the sentences and the details (the thirty-ninth chapter) in the explanation of the Beautiful Names of God
as they came by way of elucidation and details (the fortieth chapter) in the single supplications by which one is supplicated in
all the nights and times

(The first chapter concerning the dotted letters and what is in them of secrets and hidden meanings)

I say, and with God is success and guidance, the aims of the seekers have been divided into two parts, worldly and otherworldly, and each
one of them is divided into sections according to the purposes. And people have spoken concerning the opposition of the times and the standing upon the stars
and the exercises and the acts of talismans before the setting down of this book and the discourse upon it. And this science is expansive; many
creatures have desired it, and upon it especially whoever found for that a great effect. So I desired to oppose that by a description that proceeds in the manner of the sense in the aspects of
the people of this science. And the early sages spoke concerning it, and many people agreed to that saying. So those, if they are many
in the world, harm in the Hereafter. And this which I mention to you, you benefit by it in the world and the Hereafter.
(Chapter concerning the mention of the dotted letters: are they the roots of speech and its foundation, and by them its building is raised)
(And know) that for numbers there are secrets just as for letters there are effects, and that the upper world extends to the lower world; so the world of the Throne extends to the world of
the Kursi, and the world of the Kursi extends to the sphere of Saturn, and the sphere of Saturn extends to the sphere of Jupiter, and the sphere of Jupiter extends to the sphere of Mars, and the sphere of Mars
extends to the sphere of the Sun, and the sphere of the Sun extends to the sphere of Venus, and the sphere of Venus extends to the sphere of Mercury, and the sphere of Mercury extends to the sphere of the Moon,
and the sphere of the Moon extends to the sphere of heat, and the sphere of heat extends to the sphere of moisture, and the sphere of moisture extends to the sphere of cold, and the sphere of
cold extends to the sphere of dryness, and the sphere of dryness extends to the sphere of air, and the sphere of air extends to the sphere of water, and the sphere of water extends to the sphere of
earth, and the sphere of earth extends to the sphere of Saturn. In the upper things is the letter jim, and the numbers falling upon it are three
in general; and as for in detail, then fifty-three, thus the mim with four and the ba with three. And it
also is with three letters, and for it among the lower things is the letter sad, and it is in number ninety, and those in the upper things in general are five,
and it is the letter ha. And for it among the fivefold accords and the sphere of Jupiter is six, and it is the letter waw. And for it among the sixfold accords
(And as for) the inflection of the sphere of Venus, for it is the letter zay, and for it among the sevenfold accords. And as for the inflection of the sphere of Mercury, for it is
among the number eight, and it is the letter ha, and for it among the eightfold accords. (And as for) the inflection of the sphere of the Moon, for it is among the number
nine, and it is the letter ta, and for it among the ninefold accords. And Saturn has the triangle famous among the scholars, so understand that.
(Chapter concerning the relation of the human essence)
So the Throne has the letter alif, and the Kursi has the letter ba, and Saturn has the letter jim, and thus to the Moon as has preceded.
(Chapter) And the letters are of kinds: among them is that by which one begins on the right, and they are the letters of the Arabs; and among them is that by which one begins
on the left, and they are the Roman, the Greek, and the Coptic. And every writing on the right is connected, and every writing on the left
is separate, so understand that. (And the letters) are twenty-eight, other than lam-alif, and it is the completion of the twenty-nine, and that is
the number of the lunar mansions. And when the mansions, from which fourteen appear above the earth, were such, these letters
among them are what is assimilated with the lam of the definition, and they are fourteen letters: t th d dh r z s sh s d t z l n

## Page 12

**Arabic (original):**
— ٦ —

ومنها ما يظهر معها وهي أربعة عشر حرفاً كما ترى: أ ب ج ح ك م ع غ ف ق هـ و ي. وأول
الحروف الألف وما بعدها من الحروف كالطاعات والتعريفات والراءات وهي من جانب الألف وما
بعدها، فإذا نظر ناظر إلى الحروف وجدها انطباقاً في النفس قبل وجودها في الشكل فافهم، فالألف
في الحروف هي الواحد في الأعداد، والأعداد قوة روحانية لطيفة، فالاعتماد عليه ذلك من أسرار
الأقوال كما أن الحروف من أسرار الأفعال، وللأعداد في عالم البشر أسرار ومنافع رتبها جلت قدرته
كارتيب في الحروف أسرار النفع كالدعاء والرقى وغير ذلك مما يظهر تأثيره للعالم بأنواع الأسماء. (واعلم)
أن الحروف لا وقت لها يحصرها وإنما هي تفعل بالرياضة والخاصية لمن شاء، والأعداد تفعل بالطلسمات
فهي مرتبطة بالاعتبارات العلويات. (فحرف الدال) له من الأعداد أربعة، فمن أقام شكلاً ضرب
٤ في ٤ ووضع فيه نسبة يوم الاثنين يوم ولد النبي صلى الله عليه وسلم ويوم مبعثه ويوم
وفاته في شرف القمر على ثلاث درج من الثور سالماً من النحوس، ولتكن الساعة للقمر وتكتبه
بعد طهارة كاملة وصلاة ركعتين بآية الكرسي والإخلاص مائة مرة في رق طاهر، فمن حمل هذا
الوفق معه يسر الله تعالى له الحفظ والفهم ويعظم قدره عند العالم العلوي والسفلي أجمع، وإذا حمله
مسجون تخلص بإذن الله تعالى، ومن حمله على راية هزم الأعداء من الكفرة والباغين، ومن حمله
وخاصم به أحداً فإنه يغلبه ويقهره، وأعداده الواقعة عليه ٤ في ٤ وشكله من ضرب ٤ في ٤
وهو للعناصر الأربعة النار والهواء والتراب والماء وهي الصفراء والبلغم والدم والسوداء، فهذه
أربعة لا ربعة، فله قوة الطبائع واعتدالها، وظهر هذا الحرف الكريم في اسمه تعالى الدائم خصوصاً
وفي اسمه الودود، ولم يتقدم في الدائم غير الدال، ولذلك كان الاسمين الشريفين الكريمين أحمد
ومحمد، وهو يشير إلى أن الدوام آخر المنتهى لأوله فهو بعد الدال للدوام، وإنما تقدمت في اسمه الدائم
لأن له الديمومة أولاً وآخراً، فأشرك عباده في دوام البقاء بعد الفناء في الآخرة، وهذا الحرف للعرش
لأن العرش لا يتبدل وجوده لأنه أول عالم الاختراعات وهو أول عالم وإليه معارج الأرواح
وفيه مراتب العقول وفيه أنوار الرحمة، وقد كشف ذلك بعض العارفين بالله تعالى على القسم الذي قسم
له حارثة رضي الله تعالى عنه حين سأله رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم فقال: كيف أصبحت يا حارثة؟
فقال حارثة: يا رسول الله أصبحت مؤمناً حقاً، فقال رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم: ما حقيقة إيمانك؟
فقال حارثة: أصبحت وقد عرضت نفسي على الدنيا وقد استوى عندي حجرها وذهبها وحيها وميتها
وغنيها وفقيرها، وكأني أنظر إلى عرش الرحمن بارزاً والناس يساقون إلى الحساب وإلى الجنة وإلى
النار، فقال له النبي صلى الله عليه وسلم: قد عرفت فالزم. وقال النبي صلى الله عليه وسلم في الأرواح:
إذا بانت على طهارة من الوضوء فإنها تثبت ساجدة تحت العرش. (وحرف الدال) له من الأسرار
الديمومة والبقاء. (فأما الودود) فاسم من الود والود مشترك وهو ظاهر الحب والحب باطن الود
وأول الود المحبة، والود ينقسم إلى قسمين ظاهر وباطن، فظاهره الود وباطنه الحب، فالود مسكنه
القلب وهو أكشف عوالم القلب، والشق لطيفة بين الحب والود مسكنه الشغف وأحب باطن العشق
ومسكنه الفؤاد، لأن القلب له ثلاث تجويفات إحداها في أعلاه مما غلظ منه وهو نور ساطع وهو
محل

**English:**
— 6 —

And among them is what appears with it, and they are fourteen letters as you see: أ ب ج ح ك م ع غ ف ق هـ و ي. And the first
of the letters, alif, and what comes after it of the letters, like the طاعات and the تعريفات and the راءات, and they are from the side of alif and what
comes after it. So if a beholder looks at the letters, he finds them an accord in the soul before their existence in the form, so understand. For alif
among the letters is the one among the numbers, and the numbers are a subtle spiritual power. So reliance upon it is that from the secrets of
sayings, just as the letters are from the secrets of actions. And for the numbers in the world of mankind are secrets and benefits, He, glorious is His power,
has arranged them as an arrangement in the letters; secrets of benefit, like supplication and ruqyah and other than that, what its effect appears to the world by kinds of names. (And know)
that the letters have no time that confines them; rather, they act by discipline and property for whom He wills, and the numbers act by talismans,
so they are connected to the higher considerations. (So the letter dal) has from the numbers four; so whoever establishes a Shakl, let him multiply
4 by 4 and place in it the proportion of Monday, the day the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم was born, and the day of his mission, and the day
of his death, in the exaltation of the moon on three degrees of Taurus, safe from the inauspiciousnesses; and let the hour be for the moon, and write it
after complete purity and a prayer of two rak‘ahs with Ayat al-Kursi and al-Ikhlas one hundred times on pure parchment. So whoever carries this
Wafq with him, Allah تعالى makes easy for him memorization and understanding, and his rank is عظيماً with the upper and lower world altogether. And if a prisoner carries it,
he is released, by the permission of Allah تعالى. And whoever carries it
on a banner, he defeats the enemies from the disbelievers and the transgressors. And whoever carries it
and disputes with someone by means of it, then he overcomes him and subdues him. And its numbers falling upon it are 4 by 4, and its form is from the multiplication of 4 by 4,
and it is for the four elements: fire, air, earth, and water, and they are yellow bile, phlegm, blood, and black bile. So these
are four, not fourfold; so it has the power of the natures and their equilibrium. And this noble letter appeared in His تعالى name al-Da'im especially
and in His name al-Wadud. And nothing preceded in al-Da'im except dal, and for that reason the two noble, generous names were Ahmad
and Muhammad. And it indicates that دوام is the last end of its beginning, so it is after dal for دوام. Rather, it was advanced in His name al-Da'im
because He has الديمومة first and last, so He made His servants partners in the دوام of remaining after annihilation in the Hereafter. And this letter is for the Throne
because the Throne does not change its existence, since it is the first world of inventions, and it is the first world, and to it are the ascents of the spirits,
and in it are the ranks of the intellects, and in it are the lights of mercy. And some of those who know Allah تعالى have disclosed that upon the oath which was sworn
for Harithah رضي الله تعالى عنه when the Messenger of Allah صلى الله عليه وسلم asked him and said: How have you أصبحْتَ, O Harithah?
So Harithah said: O Messenger of Allah, I have أصبحْتُ a true believer. So the Messenger of Allah صلى الله عليه وسلم said: What is the reality of your faith?
So Harithah said: I أصبحْتُ and I had presented myself to the world, and its stone and its gold, its living and its dead,
its rich and its poor, were equal to me, and as though I were looking at the Throne of the Merciful exposed, while the people are being driven to the reckoning and to Paradise and to
the Fire. So the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم said to him: You have known, so adhere. And the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم said concerning the spirits:
if they become manifest upon purity from ablution, then they remain prostrating beneath the Throne. (And the letter dal) has from the secrets
permanence and remaining. (As for al-Wadud) it is a name from love, and love is shared, and it is the outward love, and love is the inward of love,
and the first of love is affection. And love is divided into two parts, outward and inward; its outward is love and its inward is affection. So love, its dwelling is
the heart, and it is the most revealing of the worlds of the heart, and al-shaqq is a subtlety between love and love; its dwelling is al-shaghaf, and the most beloved inward of passion
and its dwelling is the fu'ād, because the heart has three cavities, one of them in its upper part, from what is thickened of it, and it is a shining light, and it is
the place

## Page 13

**Arabic (original):**
— ٧ —

محل الإسلام ومعاني الحروف هنالك مشكلة، وهو أيضاً محل القوة الناطقة في الإنسان المدبرة لمعاني
الإرادة المنبعثة من النفس، والثانية في وسط القلب وهي محل التفكر والتذكر وهو نور ساطع وهو
محل السكينة ومحل الخيال فيما تلقيه الروح، والثالثة في آخره وهي أرقه وألطفه ويعبر عنها بالفؤاد وهي
محل الإيمان والعقل والنور والتصرف والأسرار وميزان العقل ولطائف الحكم ومحل حياة
الطبيعة من الحرارة اللطيفة، ولهذا الفؤاد عين نورانية يدرك بها حقائق الملكوتيات وأسرار العلويات
الجزئيات والكليات وموازين الحقائق وهي محل الأنوار الوهبيات وأسرار العلويات، وتلك البصيرة
التي ينظر بها وهي التي قال الله تعالى فيها ﴿فَإِنَّهَا لَا تَعْمَى الْأَبْصَارُ وَلَكِن تَعْمَى الْقُلُوبُ الَّتِي فِي الصُّدُورِ﴾
والتجويف الوسط الذي هو محل العشق عين نورانية بها يدرك الطلب ومنها ينبعث الجد في الطلب
والشوق إلى الشيء المطلوب، وهي أسرع تعلقاً بالأشخاص للطافتها وبها ينكشف عالم الملك وما حواه
من صنع الله تعالى وبها يقع الاستحسان للمستحسنات، ثم للتجويف الأول عين نورانية ينظر بها إلى
أسرار المحسوسات وأطوار المركبات وحقائق الحروف وأسرارها وعظيم ما أودعه الله تعالى فيها من
أسرار الأسماء وحقائق معارفها وبه كان وهباً لعباد الله تعالى ولتعظيم الله تعالى بمعرفتها به وبما أنعم الله
عليها به من كشفها أسرار المحسوسات، وتلك بصائر القلوب كلها إلا أنهم متباينون في اختلاف الأمور
وقد تقدم في مواقيت البصائر ولطائف السرائر أن أرواح الوحي في كتاب الله ثلاثة: روح الأمين
وروح القدس وروح الأمر، فالروح الأمين يدل على التجويفة الأولى لأنها البرزخية التي
بين النطق واللسان فهو أول مراتب الوحي في التنزيل كل بما قسم الله له من الإلهام والوحي على
القلوب، وبعده روح القدس وهو أنوار ما يرد في اللوح المحفوظ إلى المرتبة الثانية من القلب فتثبت
الإيمان والبصيرة الفكرية وتظهر أنوار الحكم وأنوار الموارد واللطائف الإيمانية، ثم المرتبة
الثانية وهو محل الأقدس وهو محل السمع أيضاً وهو محل العقل، قال تعالى لنبيه صلى الله عليه
وسلم ﴿إِنَّكَ لَا تُسْمِعُ الْمَوْتَى وَلَا تُسْمِعُ الصُّمَّ الدُّعَاءَ﴾ وذلك لم يرد به موت الحس وإنما أراد به موت
الكفر والعصيان، ولم يرد بالصمم عن الآذان لأن حاسة السمع موجودة وإنما أراد بهذا السمع الذي
في الفؤاد وهو محل العقل وهو محل تنزل روح الأمر الذي يشير إلى التمكن وحقيقة الجمع، وما اختص
بهذا التنزيل إلا محمد صلى الله عليه وسلم، وقد شرحنا أدوية القلوب وخزائنها وأنوارها وبصائرها في
كتابنا المسمى بمواقف الغايات في أسرار الرياضات فراجعه تجده محكاً إن شاء الله تعالى، ﴿قال تعالى﴾
﴿إِنَّ الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا وَعَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ سَيَجْعَلُ لَهُمُ الرَّحْمَنُ وُدًّا﴾ أي فيوجد في قلوبهم وداً آخذاً بمجامعها
فيودونه، وذلك أنهم يودون قلوبهم بأنواع الأذكار والأطوار القرآت فلا يتركون من أعمال القلب ما لا
ينصرون به، ولا يودون أنفسهم إلا بقطع العوائد والمألوفات إلى أن يحصل لها ود من الله تعالى
فينقلب حديثها نطق حكمة وحركتها ارتقاء درج، ويود روحه بالحقائق الإيمانية والدقائق الإسلامية
ونور الأسرار الشرعية والأنوار الدينية إلى أن يظهر على الروح آثار الود فينظر للمعاد كشافاً ما أعد
الله تعالى من أنواع النعيم لأوليائه والعذاب لأعدائه فيتزايد طبعها في طلب الرجعة إلى الله تعالى
والاشتياق إليه ويزيد عقله في التفكر في المصنوعات فيما أودع الله من أسرار آياته ويوده بترك جميع

**English:**
— 7 —

The place of Islam and the meanings of the letters, there is a problem there, and it is also the place of the speaking power in the human being, managing the meanings of the will emanating from the soul, and the second in the middle of the heart, and it is the place of reflection and remembrance, and it is a shining light, and it is the place of tranquility and the place of imagination in what the spirit casts, and the third in its end, and it is its most delicate and most subtle, and it is expressed by the fu'ād, and it is the place of faith and intellect and light and disposition and secrets and the balance of intellect and the subtleties of wisdom and the place of the life of nature from the subtle heat, and for this fu'ād there is a luminous eye by which it perceives the realities of the malakūtī things and the secrets of the higher things, the particulars and the universals and the balances of realities, and it is the place of the gifted lights and the secrets of the higher things, and that insight by which it looks, and it is that concerning which God, تعالى, said: ﴿فَإِنَّهَا لَا تَعْمَى الْأَبْصَارُ وَلَكِن تَعْمَى الْقُلُوبُ الَّتِي فِي الصُّدُورِ﴾ and the middle cavity, which is the place of love, is a luminous eye by which the seeking is perceived, and from it earnestness in the seeking and longing for the sought thing is stirred, and it is quicker in attachment to persons because of its subtlety, and by it the world of mulk and what it contains of the making of God, تعالى, is uncovered, and by it approval occurs for the approved things, then for the first cavity there is a luminous eye by which one looks to the secrets of the sensible things and the states of the composites and the realities of the letters and their secrets and the عظیم what God, تعالى, deposited in them of the secrets of the names and the realities of their knowledges, and by it there was a gift to the servants of God, تعالى, and for magnifying God, تعالى, by knowing Him through it and by what God bestowed upon it of its uncovering the secrets of the sensible things, and those are the insights of all the hearts, except that they are differing in the اختلاف of matters. And there has preceded in the times of the insights and the subtleties of the inner secrets that the spirits of revelation in the Book of God are three: the Spirit of the Trustworthy One, and the Spirit of Holiness, and the Spirit of the Command. So the Spirit of the Trustworthy One يدل upon the first cavity because it is the isthmian one that is between speech and the tongue, so it is the first of the ranks of revelation in the sending down, each according to what God apportioned for him of inspiration and revelation upon the hearts. And after it is the Spirit of Holiness, and it is the lights of what comes down in the Preserved Tablet to the second rank of the heart, so faith and the intellectual insight are established, and the lights of wisdom and the lights of the resources and the faith subtleties appear. Then the second rank, and it is the place of the Most Holy, and it is also the place of hearing, and it is the place of intellect. He, تعالى, said to His Prophet, صلى الله عليه وسلم: ﴿إِنَّكَ لَا تُسْمِعُ الْمَوْتَى وَلَا تُسْمِعُ الصُّمَّ الدُّعَاءَ﴾ and that was not intended by it to mean the death of the sense, but rather He intended by it the death of unbelief and disobedience, and He did not intend by deafness the ears, because the faculty of hearing is present, but rather He intended by this hearing which is in the fu'ād, and it is the place of intellect, and it is the place of the descent of the Spirit of the Command, which indicates enabling and the reality of union, and none was singled out by this descent except Muhammad, صلى الله عليه وسلم. And we have explained the medicines of the hearts and their treasuries and their lights and their insights in our book called Mawaqif al-Ghayat fi Asrar al-Riyadat, so review it; you will find it tested, if God, تعالى, wills. ﴿He, تعالى, said﴾ ﴿إِنَّ الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا وَعَمِلُوا الصَّالِحَاتِ سَيَجْعَلُ لَهُمُ الرَّحْمَنُ وُدًّا﴾ that is, there will be found in their hearts an affection taking hold of their totalities, so they love Him. And that is because they love their hearts with the kinds of remembrances and the states of the recitations, so they do not leave from the works of the heart that by which they are not supported, and they do not love their selves except by cutting off habits and familiar things until there is obtained for it affection from God, تعالى, then its speech turns into the speaking of wisdom and its movement into the ascent of degrees, and he loves his spirit with the realities of faith and the الدقيقة الإسلامية and the light of the الشرعية secrets and the religious lights until the آثار of affection appear upon the spirit, so it looks to the return, unveiling what God, تعالى, has prepared of kinds of bliss for His friends and punishment for His enemies, so its nature increases in seeking the return to God, تعالى, and longing for Him, and its intellect increases in reflection upon the made things in what God deposited of the secrets of His signs, and he loves it by leaving all

## Page 14

**Arabic (original):**
- ٨ -

العلائق والمألوفات ويوده بامتثال أمره وهو على الطهارة ولا يأمر إلا بالخير النافع، فإذا توجه القلب إلى الود عاد ناظراً في أسرار عجائب الملكوت، فأطوار المخاطبات والوحي الإلهي والحقائق العلويات. ولنرجع إلى ما كنا بصدده من حرف الدال وفوائده، من كتب حرف الدال خمسة وثلاثين مرة وهو عدد الواقع عليه، وكتب معه شكل المربع على حريرة بيضاء والقمر في بيته محفوظاً من المشتري، وحولها حرف الدال خمسة وثلاثين مرة، وضعها في جوف الخاتم في ذلك الوقت ويلبسه على طهارة كاملة وصوم وصفاء باطن، أدام الله تعالى عليه الرزق والخير، ومن أكثر من ذكر اسمه الدائم حصل له ذلك، وقد ذكرنا جملة من خواص اسمه الدائم والدال من الحمد في كتابنا "علم الهدى وأسرار الاهتدا" فراجعه. (ومن رسمه) على هذه الصفة وحمله معه نال جميع المقاصد عند الملوك والحكام وأحبه من رآه ونال الحظ الوافر، وهذه كتابته في حريرة صفراء ويكون القمر في بيت السرطان أو في بيت المشتري محفوظاً منه، وبخره بكل ذي رائحة طيبة وهذه صفته.

(وذكر بعضهم) أن من كتب محمد رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم أحمد رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم خمسة وثلاثين مرة في بطاقة بعد صلاة الجمعة وحمله معه رزقه الله تعالى قوة على الطاعة ومعونة على البركة وكفاه همزات الشياطين، وإن أدام النظر إلى هذه البطاقة وهو يتخيل اسم النبي صلى الله عليه وسلم محمد واسمه أحمد وكيف كملت الاسمان الشريفان المعظمان المكرمان المبجلان بهذا الحرف الكريم وهو الدال، ويديم النظر إليه عند طلوع الشمس في كل يوم وهو يصلي على النبي صلى الله عليه وسلم يسر الله له أسباب الطاعة والسعادة العظمى، وذلك بحسب القبول وصدق النية وصفاء الباطن وهو سر لطيف جداً. (ومن كتب) شكله العددي وحمله أمنه الله تعالى من الأعداء المضرين من أي العوالم كانوا. (ومن كتبه) ومحاه وسقاه لمن يشتكي حمى مطبقة نفعه ذلك كثيراً. (ومن) تحقق ألم السم من العقارب والحيات وشربه أمنه الله منه. (ومن كتب)

**English:**
- 8 -

The relations and the familiarities, and he loves him by complying with his command, and he is upon purity, and he commands only with the beneficial good. So when the heart turns toward love, it returns looking into the secrets of the wonders of the kingdom, then the states of the addresses, and the divine revelation, and the upper realities. And let us return to what we were engaged in, of the letter Dāl and its benefits: whoever writes the letter Dāl thirty-five times, and it is the number falling upon it, and writes with it the form of the square on a white silk cloth, and the moon in its house, محفوظاً from Jupiter, and around it the letter Dāl thirty-five times, put it in the interior of the ring at that time and wear it upon complete purity and fasting and inward clarity—may God تعالى perpetuate upon him provision and good. And whoever increases in mentioning his everlasting name, that is obtained for him. And we have mentioned a body of the properties of his everlasting name and the Dāl from praise in our book “The Science of Guidance and the Secrets of Being Guided,” so review it. (And whoever draws it) in this form and carries it with him attains all aims with the kings and the rulers, and whoever sees him loves him, and he attains the abundant fortune. And this is its writing on a yellow silk cloth, and the moon is in the house of Cancer or in the house of Jupiter, محفوظاً from it, and fumigate it with every thing of pleasant scent, and this is its description.

(And some of them mentioned) that whoever writes “Muhammad is the Messenger of God, صلى الله عليه وسلم, Ahmad is the Messenger of God, صلى الله عليه وسلم” thirty-five times on a card after the Friday prayer and carries it with him, God تعالى provides him with strength for obedience and assistance for blessing, and suffices him the stings of the devils. And if he continues looking at this card while he imagines the name of the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم Muhammad and his name Ahmad, and how the two noble, exalted, honored, venerated names were completed by this noble letter, which is the Dāl, and he continues looking at it at sunrise every day while he prays upon the Prophet صلى الله عليه وسلم, God makes easy for him the means of obedience and the greatest happiness, and that according to acceptance and truthfulness of intention and purity of inwardness, and it is a very subtle secret. (And whoever writes) its numerical form and carries it, God تعالى secures him from the harmful enemies, from whatever worlds they may be. (And whoever writes it) and erases it and gives it to drink to one who complains of an all-encompassing fever, that benefits him greatly. (And whoever) verifies the pain of the poison from scorpions and snakes and drinks it, God secures him from it. (And whoever writes)

**Arabic (original):**
— ٩ —

شَكْلُهُ الْعَدَدِيُّ وَحَمْلُهُ مَعَهُ أَمَّنَهُ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى مِنَ الْأَعْدَاءِ
الْمُضِرِّينَ مِنْ أَيِّ الْعَوَالِمِ كَانُوا وَهَذِهِ صُورَتُهُ
( وَأَمَّا الْحَرْفِيُّ ) خَاصِيَّتُهُ يَذْهَبُ النِّسْيَانَ وَيُحِدُّ الْفَهْمَ وَالْعَقْلَ
لِمَنِ اسْتَدَامَ شُرْبَهُ فِي مَاءِ الْمَطَرِ وَعَسَلِ نَحْلٍ وَيَنْفَعُ لِمَنْ يَشْتَكِي
صَدْرَهُ وَأَذَانَهُ، وَالْقَمَرُ فِي الْعَقْرَبِ وَالْمِرِّيخُ يَنْظُرُ إِلَيْهِ نَظَرَ عَدَاوَةٍ فِي لَوْحٍ مِنْ نُحَاسٍ وَيَحْمِيهِ مِنَ الدَّارِ فَهُوَ عَظِيمٌ
لِلْدَغِ الْعَقْرَبِ إِذَا سُقِيَ مَاؤُهُ بَعْدَ أَنْ يُغْمَسَ الْخَاتَمُ فِيهِ وَلَمَّا كَانَ هَذَا الشَّكْلُ الْمُرَبَّعُ بِمَجْمُوعِ الْأَلِفَاتِ الْأَرْبَعَةِ الَّتِي هِيَ
سِرُّ الْعَقْلِ وَسِرُّ الرُّوحِ وَسِرُّ النَّفْسِ وَسِرُّ الْقَلْبِ فَالْأَلِفُ فِي الْعَدَدِ وَاحِدٌ وَإِذَا ضُرِبَتِ الْأَرْبَعَةُ فِي نَفْسِهَا بَلَغَتْ سِتَّةَ
عَشَرَ وَهُوَ انْتِهَاءُ الْعَدَدِ التَّفْصِيلِيِّ لِأَنَّ الْعَرْشَ وَالْكُرْسِيَّ وَالسَّمَوَاتِ السَّبْعَ وَالْأَرَضِينَ السَّبْعَ الْجُمْلَةَ سِتَّةَ
عَشَرَ وَهَذَا الْعَدَدُ هُوَ انْتِهَاءُ هَذَا الشَّكْلِ الْمُرَبَّعِ الَّذِي هُوَ سِتَّةَ عَشَرَ يَتَأَلَّفُ السِّتَّةَ عَشَرَ شَفْعَةً الْأَرْبَعَةَ عَشَرَ
وَهِيَ السَّمَوَاتُ السَّبْعُ وَالْأَرَضُونَ السَّبْعُ وَفِيهِ شَفْعَةُ الْبُرُوجِ وَهِيَ اثْنَا عَشَرَ وَفِيهِ شَفْعَةُ الثَّمَانِيَةِ وَهُمْ حَمَلَةُ
الْعَرْشِ وَفِيهِ شَفْعَةُ السِّتَّةِ وَهِيَ الْحُدُودُ الثَّمَانِيَةُ وَهِيَ فَوْقَ وَتَحْتَ وَخَلْفَ وَأَمَامَ وَيَمِينٍ وَشِمَالٍ وَفِيهِ شَفْعَةُ
الْأَرْبَعَةِ وَهِيَ شَفْعَةُ النَّبِيِّينَ وَالصِّدِّيقِينَ وَالشُّهَدَاءِ وَالصَّالِحِينَ وَفِيهِ شَفْعَةُ الْآيَتَيْنِ وَهِيَ شَهَادَةُ أَنْ لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ
وَأَنَّ مُحَمَّدًا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ فَهَذِهِ سَبْعَةُ أَشْفَاعٍ وَفِيهِ مِنَ الْوَتَرِيَّةِ وَتَرُ الْخَمْسَةِ عَشَرَ وَهِيَ الْكُرْسِيُّ
وَالسَّمَوَاتُ وَالْأَرَضُونَ وَفِيهِ وَتَرُ الثَّلَاثَةَ عَشَرَ وَهِيَ الْقَلَمُ وَاللَّوْحُ وَالصُّورُ وَرُوحُ الْقُدُسِ وَالْكُرْسِيُّ وَالْعَرْشُ
وَالسَّمَوَاتُ السَّبْعُ وَفِيهِ وَتَرُ الْإِحْدَى عَشَرَ وَهِيَ مَا فِي عَالَمِ الْإِنْسَانِ مِنَ الْحَوَاسِّ الْخَمْسِ وَهِيَ السَّمْعُ وَالْبَصَرُ
وَالشَّمُّ وَالذَّوْقُ وَاللَّمْسُ وَالْجِهَاتُ السِّتُّ وَالْفَوْقُ وَالتَّحْتُ وَالْيَمِينُ وَالشِّمَالُ وَالْخَلْفُ وَالْأَمَامُ وَفِيهِ وَتَرِيَّةُ التِّسْعَةِ
وَهِيَ ذَوَاتُ الْإِنْسَانِ وَطَبَائِعُهُ الثَّمَانِيَةُ وَهِيَ الْحَرَارَةُ وَالْيُبُوسَةُ وَالْبُرُودَةُ وَالرُّطُوبَةُ فَالصَّفْرَاءُ حَارَّةٌ يَابِسَةٌ
وَالْهَوَاءُ حَارٌّ رَطْبٌ وَهُوَ طَبْعُ الدَّمِ وَالْبَلْغَمُ طَبْعُهُ بَارِدٌ رَطْبٌ وَالسَّوْدَاءُ بَارِدَةٌ يَابِسَةٌ فَهَذِهِ ثَمَانِيَةٌ مُنْفَصِلَةٌ
وَفِيهِ وَتَرُ السَّبْعَةِ وَهِيَ وَتَرُ الْأَفْلَاكِ السَّبْعَةِ فَلَكَ زُحَلَ وَالْمُشْتَرِي وَالْمِرِّيخِ وَالشَّمْسِ وَالزُّهْرَةِ وَعُطَارِدَ
وَالْقَمَرِ وَوَتَرُ الْأَيَّامِ السَّبْعَةِ وَالسَّمَوَاتِ وَالْأَرَضِينَ السَّبْعِ وَكُلُّ مَسْبَعٍ وَفِيهِ وَتَرُ الْأَشْفَاعِ وَهِيَ الصَّلَوَاتُ الْخَمْسُ
وَفِيهِ وَتَرُ الثَّلَاثَةِ وَهِيَ الدُّورُ الثَّلَاثَةُ دَارُ الدُّنْيَا وَدَارُ الْبَرْزَخِ وَدَارُ الْآخِرَةِ وَفِيهِ وَتَرُ الْوَاحِدِ وَهِيَ وَتَرُ الْعَقْلِ
فَاجْمَعْ فِي السِّتَّةَ عَشَرَ ثَمَانِيَةَ أَشْفَاعٍ وَثَمَانِيَةَ أَوْتَارٍ وَكُلُّ شَفْعٍ يَتَلَقَّى كَلَوْتٍ وَكَلَوْتٍ وَيَتَلَقَّى كُلُّ شَفْعٍ * مِثَالُ
ذَلِكَ وَاحِدٌ وَاحِدٌ اثْنَانِ وَثَلَاثَةٌ وَسِتَّةٌ وَهَكَذَا إِلَى آخِرِهِ وَكَذَلِكَ الْأَشْفَاعُ * وَأَمَّا الشَّكْلُ الْعَدَدِيُّ بِالْقَلَمِ
الطَّبِيعِيِّ وَهُوَ الْهِنْدِيُّ عَلَى مَا يَأْتِي بَيَانُهُ وَهَذَا الْقَلَمُ أَوْلَى بِهِ وَأَحَقُّ وَلَهُ فِي الْحُرُوفِ أَسْرَارٌ عَجِيبَةٌ وَذَلِكَ أَنْ
تَضَعَ شَكْلًا وَمَكَانَ الْأَعْدَادِ حُرُوفًا وَيَكُونُ بَعْدَ صِيَامٍ أُسْبُوعَيْنِ لِأَنَّ كُلَّ فِيهَا لَيْلًا إِلَّا الْجُزْءَ وَحْدَهُ
بِشَرْطِ الرِّيَاضَةِ وَدَوَامِ الطَّهَارَةِ وَذِكْرِ اللَّهِ تَعَالَى ثُمَّ اعْمَدْ إِلَى صَفِيحَةٍ مُرَبَّعَةٍ مِنْ قَصْدِيرٍ مُصَفَّى مَنْقُوشٍ
عَلَيْهَا هَذَا الشَّكْلُ الْحَرْفِيُّ وَأَنْتَ مُسْتَقْبِلُ الْقِبْلَةِ بَعْدَ الصَّلَاةِ رَكْعَتَيْنِ بِالْفَاتِحَةِ وَآيَةِ الْكُرْسِيِّ مَرَّةً وَالْإِخْلَاصِ
مِائَةَ مَرَّةٍ فِي سَاعَةِ الْمُشْتَرِي عِنْدَ طُلُوعِ الشَّمْسِ وَالْقَمَرُ مَحْفُوظٌ مِنَ الْمُشْتَرِي وَالشَّمْسِ وَالطَّالِعِ
الْجَوْزَاءِ وَبَخِّرْهُ بِالْمَصْطَكَى وَالصَّنْدَلِ الْأَبْيَضِ كُلَّ يَوْمٍ خَمِيسٍ فَلَابِسُ هَذَا الْخَاتَمِ يَسْهُلُ اللَّهُ لَهُ أُمُورَ الدُّنْيَا
وَالْآخِرَةِ وَالدِّيَانَةِ وَيُوَفِّقُهُ لِلْأَعْمَالِ الصَّالِحَةِ وَالطَّاعَاتِ وَتَيَسَّرَ لَهُ أَسْبَابُ الرِّزْقِ وَيُبَارِكُ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى فِيمَا بَيْنَ
يَدَيْهِ (وَمَنْ كَتَبَهُ) وَوَضَعَهُ فِي دُكَانِهِ أَوْ صُنْدُوقِهِ كَثُرَ مَالُهُ وَرِزْقُهُ وَيَكُونُ مَعَهُ كَلَامُهُ الْمَخْصُوصُ بِهِ طَلَبُ الرِّزْقِ

| د | يد | يه | ا |

| و | ب | ج | ١٦ |

**English:**
— 9 —

Its numerical form, and carrying it with him, God Most High secured him from the harmful enemies, from whichever of the worlds they were, and this is its form.

(And as for the literal one), its property is: it takes away forgetfulness and sharpens understanding and intellect for whoever continues drinking it in rainwater and honey of bees, and it benefits for whoever complains of his chest and his ears. And the moon is in Scorpio, and Mars looks toward it with a look of enmity, in a tablet of copper, and it protects him from the house, so it is عظیم for the sting of Scorpio if its water is drunk after the ring is dipped in it. And since this square form is with the total of the four alifs, which are the secret of the intellect and the secret of the spirit and the secret of the soul and the secret of the heart, then the alif in the number is one, and if the four are multiplied by themselves they reach sixteen, and it is the end of the detailed number, because the Throne and the Footstool and the seven heavens and the seven earths, the total, [is] sixteen. And this number is the end of this square form, which is sixteen; the sixteen is composed of fourteen pairs, and they are the seven heavens and the seven earths, and in it is the pair of the signs, and they are twelve, and in it is the pair of the eighth, and they are the bearers of the Throne, and in it is the pair of six, and they are the eight limits, and they are above and below and behind and before and right and left, and in it is the pair of four, and they are the pair of the prophets and the truthful and the martyrs and the righteous, and in it is the pair of the two verses, and they are the testimony that there is no god except God and that Muhammad is the Messenger of God, peace be upon him. So these are seven pairs. And in it is from the oddness the odd of fifteen, and they are the Footstool and the heavens and the earths. And in it is the odd of thirteen, and they are the Pen and the Tablet and the Sur and the Holy Spirit and the Footstool and the Throne and the seven heavens. And in it is the odd of eleven, and they are what is in the world of man of the five senses, and they are hearing and sight and smell and taste and touch, and the six directions, and above and below and right and left and behind and before. And in it is the oddness of nine, and they are the entities of man and his eight natures, and they are heat and dryness and coldness and moisture. So the yellow bile is hot, dry, and the air is hot, moist, and it is the nature of blood, and phlegm, its nature is cold, moist, and black bile is cold, dry. So these are eight separate. And in it is the odd of seven, and it is the odd of the seven spheres: the sphere of Saturn, and Jupiter, and Mars, and the Sun, and Venus, and Mercury, and the Moon; and the odd of the seven days, and the seven heavens, and the seven earths, and every sevenfold. And in it is the odd of the pairs, and they are the five prayers. And in it is the odd of three, and they are the three abodes: the abode of the world, and the abode of the barzakh, and the abode of the Hereafter. And in it is the odd of one, and it is the odd of the intellect.

So gather in the sixteen eight pairs and eight odds, and every pair receives as an odd and as an odd, and every pair receives * Example of that: one, one, two, and three, and six, and thus to its end, and likewise the pairs. * And as for the numerical form with the natural pen, and it is the Indian, according to what the explanation of it will come, and this pen is more fitting for it and more deserving, and in letters there are wondrous secrets. And that is that you place a form and in the place of the numbers letters, and it is after a fast of two weeks, because every one of them is a night except the part alone, on the condition of discipline and the دوام of purity and the remembrance of God Most High. Then proceed to a square sheet of refined tin, engraved upon it this literal form, while you are facing the qiblah, after two rak‘ahs of prayer with al-Fatiha and Ayat al-Kursi once and al-Ikhlas one hundred times, in the hour of Jupiter at sunrise, and the moon is محفوظ from Jupiter and the sun and the rising sign of Gemini. And fumigate it with mastic and white sandalwood every Thursday. So the wearer of this ring, God makes easy for him the affairs of the world and the Hereafter and religion, and He grants him success for righteous deeds and acts of obedience, and the means of provision are made easy for him, and God Most High blesses what is between his hands. (And whoever writes it) and places it in his shop or his صندوق, his wealth and his provision become abundant, and there will be with him his speech, the one specified by it: the seeking of provision.

وحصول البركة على ما يأتي بعده إن شاء الله تعالى (ومن كتبه) يوم الخميس في رق ظبي عند طلوع الشمس وحمله في مخيط ثيابه أمن بعون الله تعالى من اللصوص وجميع المكاره وكل ما يخاف ويحذر وهو أول موضوعات الأعداد وسأنبيك عن شيء من أسرار الأعداد وما أبرزه الله فيها وصفة منافعها ومضارها وتصاريفها وسر الحروف المعجمة التي في كتاب الله تعالى وهي أوائل السور التي هي ثمانية وعشرون سورة لا يطلع عليها إلا خواص خلقه وما في أسماء الله التي هي كنوز الأسرار ومجاري الأقدار ومعرفة اسم الله الأعظم الكبير الأكبر وما فيه من الأسرار الإلهية وصفات الربوبية ما لا تجده في كتاب ولا تقف عليه في ديوان مما جمعته من درر العلوم وغوامض الأسرار حتى ينتفع به من يقرؤه ويفهم معناه إن شاء الله تعالى وهذه صورته وصفته كما ترى وله دعاء عظيم منظوم من شكله وحروفه أ ب ج د هـ و ز ح ط ي زيد عليها الياء وهو العاشر وانتظم منها هذه الدعوة الشريفة وهي هذه:
اللهم إني أسألك بأسمائك الحسنى كلها ما علمت منها وما لم أعلم يا هو يا واحد يا أحد يا هادي يا رب يا باري يا بصير يا بديع يا باسط يا باقي يا جليل يا دائم يا وارث يا ودود يا حي يا حكيم يا حليم يا ظاهر يا مظهر أجب دعوتي واقض حاجتي يا رب العالمين (ولما تقدم) أن الحروف ثمانية وعشرون على عدد المنازل الثمانية والعشرين كان الظاهر منها فوق الأرض أربعة عشر منزلة وتحت الأرض أربعة عشر فإذا كانت منزلة طلعت الخامسة عشر نظيرتها وهكذا أبداً ولذلك كانت الحروف خمسة عشر منقطة وأربعة عشر بلا نقط والممنقطة هكذا: ب ث ج خ ذ ز ش ض ظ غ ف ق ن، وغير المنقوطة هكذا: ا ح د ر س ص ط ع ك ل م هـ و لا. (واعلم) وفقنا الله وإياك أن الحروف الغير المنقوطة منازل السعودات والمنقوطة منازل النحوسات وما كان له نقطة واحدة كان أقرب إلى السعد وما كان له نقطتان كان متوسطاً في النحوسات وما كان له ثلاث كان نحساً أكبر مثلها الشين والتاء فتدبر ذلك. (واعلم) أن المنازل لها أشكال مختلفة الوضع في الحلقة الإلهية لا تشبه بعضها بعضاً والقمر خلقه الله مستديراً وكذلك الشمس لسر خفي لا يمكن شرحه لأن إفشاء سر الربوبية كفر، فالقمر إذا نزل بمنزلة النطح كان له إشارات تدل عليه وكلام طويل سيأتي بعضه ولكن للحيطان آذان والأحسن في هذا الكتمان فافهم ما أشرت به وتدبره ترشد.

( الفصل الثاني في الكسر والبسط وترتيب الأعمال في الأوقات والساعات )
( اعلم ) وفقني الله وإياك لطاعته وفهم أسرار أسمائه أن الشمس والقمر ذكرهما الله تعالى في كتابه العزيز بقوله تعالى: كل في فلك يسبحون، وذلك أن القمر إذا كان بمنزلة النطح كان له حرف الألف وكان بسر الألف فإذا نزل المنزلة فيتجلى من تلك المنزلة روحانية الألف فيظهر الغضب في أجزاء العالم وأكثره في أشراف أهل الدنيا وأكبرها فيجد كل أحد من الخلق القهر والغضب في باطنه على النوع الذي فيه تفقد ذلك وجده فينبغي للإنسان أن يسكن في تلك الساعة ويشغل جوارحه في عبادة الله تعالى وكثرة الدعاء والذكر ولزوم الطهارة في تلك المدة.

**English:**
— 10 —

And the obtaining of blessing upon what comes after it, if God Most High wills. And (whoever writes it) on Thursday on gazelle parchment at sunrise and carries it in the sewn seam of his clothes, he is secured, by the help of God Most High, from thieves and all afflictions and everything that is feared and guarded against. And it is the first of the subjects of numbers, and I shall inform you of something of the secrets of numbers and what God has manifested in them, and the description of their benefits and harms and their transformations, and the secret of the dotted letters that are in the Book of God Most High, and they are the beginnings of the surahs, which are twenty-eight surahs, upon which none looks except the elect of His creation, and what is in the names of God, which are the treasuries of secrets and the courses of decrees, and the knowledge of the name of God, the greatest, the grandest, and what is in it of the divine secrets and the attributes of lordship, what you will not find in a book nor come upon in a register, from what I have gathered of the pearls of the sciences and the obscure secrets, so that he who reads it and understands its meaning may benefit by it, if God Most High wills. And this is its form and its description, as you see. And it has a great supplication composed from its form and its letters: ا ب ج د هـ و ز ح ط ي, the yā’ being added to it, and it is the tenth, and from them this noble invocation was arranged, and it is this:

O God, I ask You by all Your most beautiful names, what I know of them and what I do not know: O He, O One, O Single, O Guide, O Lord, O Creator, O All-Seeing, O Inventive, O Expander, O Remaining, O Majestic, O Everlasting, O Inheritor, O Loving, O Living, O Wise, O Forbearing, O Manifest, O One who makes manifest, answer my supplication and fulfill my need, O Lord of the worlds. (And since it has preceded) that the letters are twenty-eight according to the number of the twenty-eight mansions, the apparent of them above the earth is fourteen mansions and beneath the earth fourteen; so when a mansion has risen, its fifteenth counterpart has risen, and thus forever. Therefore the letters are fifteen dotted and fourteen without dots. And the dotted ones are thus: ب ث ج خ ذ ز ش ض ظ غ ف ق ن, and the undotted ones are thus: ا ح د ر س ص ط ع ك ل م هـ و لا. (And know) may God grant us and you success, that the undotted letters are the mansions of the fortunes and the dotted letters are the mansions of the misfortunes, and what has one dot is closer to fortune, and what has two dots is intermediate in the misfortunes, and what has three is a greater misfortune, like the shīn and the tā’, so reflect on that. (And know) that the mansions have different forms of placement in the divine circle, not resembling one another, and the moon God created round, and likewise the sun, for a hidden secret that cannot be explained, because divulging the secret of lordship is unbelief. So when the moon descends in the mansion of al-naṭḥ, it has indications that point to it, and a long discourse, some of which will come, but walls have ears, and the best thing in this is concealment, so understand what I have indicated and reflect on it, you will be guided.

(Chapter Two on breaking and expansion and the arrangement of works in the times and hours)
( Know ) may God grant me and you success in obedience to Him and understanding the secrets of His names, that the sun and the moon God Most High mentioned in His Mighty Book by His saying, Most High: each in an orbit are swimming. That is because the moon, when it is in the mansion of al-naṭḥ, has the letter alif, and it is with the secret of alif. So when it descends the mansion, the spirituality of alif becomes manifest from that mansion, and anger appears in the parts of the world, and most of it is in the nobles of the people of the world, and greatest of it, so every one of creation finds coercion and anger in his inward according to the kind in which you inspect that, you find it. So it is fitting for a person to be still in that hour and occupy his limbs in the worship of God Most High and much supplication and remembrance and adherence to purity during that period.

And say:

وقبل المدة لأنه يحصل فيها بعض تنغيص النفوس حتى لا يدرى الإنسان ما سبب قبضه ويصير
متعجباً في نفسه وذلك أن الألف هي أول مراتب الآحاد في الأعداد والحروف فلا شبهة له فيها
ولذلك وقع الانزعاج في العالم السفلي فافهم ذلك وفيه تنغيص من أردت تنغيصه وقبضه من أصحاب
الدنيا من أهل التجبر والتكبر فإنه يناسب تنغيصه وقبضه لما في حرف الألف من الحرارة واليبوسة
وهو وجه الأحمر والأحمر حار يابس طبيعته النار محرق محبس فإذا دعوت فيه بأسماء حارة يابسة من
طبعه إذا كان القمر في النطح طالعاً على الأفق الشرقي والقمر فيه ويصح ما ذكر ﵆ ومن كتب
حرف الألف مائة مرة وإحدى عشرة مرة في نحاس أحمر أو حديد أو شقف فخار أهر على اسم
من أردت تنغيصه وادفنه في داره بعد تبخيره من جنسه ويكون فيه الحرارة مثل الحرف مما
يناسبها وادع بالأسماء مائة وإحدى عشرة مرة وهي الأعداد الواقعة عليه وذلك أن تأخذ حروف
اسم من أردت تنغيصه وابسطها وانظر الغالب عليه في اسمه من الطبائع الأربعة وهي الحرارة واليبوسة
والبرودة والرطوبة فتأخذ تلك الحروف الحارة واليابسة من اسمه وتضعها بين يديك في لوح
وتضيف إليه حروف المريخ والقمر وتجعل منها اسماً من أسماء الله تعالى وتدعو بها العدد
المذكور واجمع همتك في فمه وقهره يكون ذلك ﵆ مثاله زيد وعمرو فضع الحروف هكذا م ر و
م د ي خ ن ط ح ق م ر مقطعة مبسوطة فهذه أربعة عشر حرفاً منها ناري وهوائي وترابي ومائي
وهي و ي ن ومن الحروف الرطبة حرف واحد وهو حرف ق فكانت الحروف الحارة المكررة
أربعة م م ط واليابسة حروف وهي ح ع د د ح والحارة ثلاثة أحرف والرطبة واحد
فانحصرت الأربعة عشر حرفاً ههنا فكان الغالب على هذه الحروف الحرارة وحر اليبوسة فخرج
لنا من أسماء الله تعالى هذه العزيمة تقول يا سمسائيل بالذي خلقك فسواك وجعلك
نوراً في فلكه إلا ما كنت عدتي فإني سلطتك على ٦٥٢ وعوناً لي فيما أريد من الانتقام من كذا
وكذا وفقد حواسه وتمزج بحرارة المريخ في حرارة طبعه وتهيج فيه حرارة النار بقمع أوصاله
وتقبض بها على بطنه وتلف بها عقله وتترك عليه العذاب ونار المريخ وتحرك النيران
والصداع وسائر الأوجاع بحق المريخ وما فيه من نحس ونار وبحق منزلتك العالية المقدار اليابسة
الحارة المنتقمة من الظلمة الطاغين والباغين وأرسل إليه روحانية هذا الخيار الطاغي المتكبر الباغي
وسكنوا في جسمه من عذاب الأسقام وسلطوا على باطنه القهر والغضب والانتقام فإني أقسمت
عليكم بالقوي المحيط الظاهر الحي القيوم النور المؤمن المقدم المؤخر مفيض الأنوار ومعطي الأسرار
وبحق النار والشرار والكوكب الأحمر وبحق الله الواحد القهار أجيبوا طائعين مسرعين لأسماء
رب العالمين ( المنزلة الأولى ) الشرطين وله حرف الألف وله وفق طواعي وكوكبه المريخ وخادمه
الأحمر وهو حرف قوي الفعال إذا ضربته في مثله أظهر الطاعة وهو نهاية الآحاد ( واعلم ) أن هذا

يكتب كابايناه في ساعة سعيدة وإن مزجت اسم الشخص الذي تريد العمل له مع حروف الوفق
كان أجمل وأقوى في الأفعال وهذه العزيمة تقول أقسمت عليك يا سمسائيل وخدمتك وأعوانك

**English:**
— 11 —

And before the period, because in it there occurs some embittering of souls, so that the human being does not know what is the cause of his constriction, and he becomes
wondering within himself. And that is because the alif is the first rank of the ones in numbers and letters, so there is no doubt in it
for him. And therefore disturbance occurred in the lower world, so understand that. And in it is embittering of whomever you want to embitter and constrict, from the people
of the world, from the people of domineering and arrogance, for it suits his embittering and constriction, because in the letter alif there is heat and dryness,
and it is the face of the red one, and the red one is hot, dry; his nature is fire, burning, imprisoning. So if you call in it with hot, dry names from
its nature, when the moon is in al-Natḥ, rising on the eastern horizon, and the moon is in it, and what was mentioned is correct ﵆. And whoever writes
the letter alif one hundred times and eleven times in red copper or iron or a potsherd of pottery, cast it upon the name
of whomever you want to embitter, and bury it in his house after fumigating it with something of its kind, and there should be in it heat like the letter, from what
corresponds to it, and call with the names one hundred and eleven times, and they are the numbers falling upon it. That is, you take the letters
of the name of whomever you want to embitter, and spread them out, and look at what predominates over him in his name from the four natures, which are heat and dryness,
coldness and moisture. Then you take those hot and dry letters from his name and place them before you in a tablet,
and add to them the letters of Mars and the Moon, and make from them a name from the names of God تعالى, and call by it the number
mentioned, and gather your resolve in his mouth, and his subjugation will be that ﵆. Its example: Zayd and ʿAmr, so place the letters thus: m r w
m d y kh n ṭ ḥ q m r, separated, spread out. So these are fourteen letters, among them fiery, airy, earthy, and watery,
and they are w y n. And among the moist letters there is one letter, and it is the letter q. So the repeated hot letters were
four: m m ṭ. And the dry letters are letters, namely ḥ ع d d ḥ. And the hot are three letters, and the moist one.
Thus the fourteen letters were confined here, so the predominant over these letters was heat and the heat of dryness. So there emerged
for us from the names of God تعالى this ʿazīmah: you say, O Samsāʾīl, by Him who created you, then fashioned you, and made you
a light in His sphere, except that you were my provision, for I have empowered you over 652 and a helper to me in what I desire of vengeance against so-and-so
and so-and-so, and the loss of his senses, and you mix with the heat of Mars in the heat of his nature, and you stir in him the heat of fire by subduing his joints
and you constrict with it upon his belly, and you wrap with it his intellect, and you leave upon him the torment and the fire of Mars, and you move the fires
and the headache and the rest of the pains, by the right of Mars and what is in it of ill-omen and fire, and by the right of your lofty, high-valued station, dry,
hot, avenging from the الظلمة, the tyrannical and the transgressors. And send to him the Ruhāniyyah of this chosen one, the tyrannical, the arrogant, the transgressor,
and settle in his body from the torment of ailments, and empower over his inward part subjugation, wrath, and vengeance, for I have sworn
upon you by the powerful, the encompassing, the manifest, the living, the self-subsisting, the light, the faithful, the advancing, the delaying, the effuser of lights, and the giver of secrets,
and by the right of fire and sparks and the red star, and by the right of God, the One, the Subduer: respond, obedient, hastening, to the names
of the Lord of the worlds. (The first descent) al-Sharṭayn, and it has the letter alif, and it has a compliant wafq, and its planet is Mars, and its servant
is the red one, and it is a letter of strong efficacy; if you strike it in its like, it manifests obedience, and it is the end of the ones. (And know) that this
noble letter has power in the disposition of the rest of the letters, because it is like the alif, so understand. (And among its properties) for love:
write kābāynāh in a fortunate hour, and if you mix the name of the person for whom you want to work with the letters of the wafq,
it is more beautiful and stronger in the acts. And this ʿazīmah says: I have sworn upon you, O Samsāʾīl, and your servants and your helpers

## Page 18

**Arabic (original):**
— ١٢ —

من العلوية والسفلية وخدام حرف الألف جميعا إلا ما سمعتم وأطعتم وهيجتم كذا وكذا وبحق ما أقسمت به عليكم وبحق حرف الألف وما أنزل الله فيه من الأسرار التي لا يطلع عليها أحد إلا العارفون بالله تعالى وبحق أبجد وما فيها من الخواص إلا ما أجبتم بالطاعة كما دعوتكم إليه وبما أقسمت به عليكم وهذه صورته كما ترى فافهم ترشد

وقس على هذا ما يناسبه من الأعمال والأفعال ينجح عملك والله الموفق للصواب
وإن أردت لأعمال الفساد فافعل كذكرنا أولاً من تبغض وهلاك ودمار وما أشبه ذلك وقس عليه وافعل تنل المقصود والله يقول الحق وهو يهدي السبيل

وهذه دعوة حرف الألف التي تدعو بها العدد المتقدم سبحانك لا إله إلا أنت يارب كل شيء ووارثه والأرض

(المنزلة الثانية) منزلة البطين وهو لحرف الباء إذا نزل القمر بها ينحدر منه بأمر الله تعالى قوة روحانية تصلح للغضب وما تقدم ذكره فيه يشرب الدواء ويتحرك فيه الأكابر وأبناء الدنيا وملوك الأرض لأنه الوجه الثاني من الحمل وهو وجه الشمس وفيه يكون شرفها في تسعة عشر درجة منه يوم أربعة من أبريل والشمس سعيدة إلا أنها حارة يابسة فسعدها وشرفها في هذا الوجه تعمل فيه للقبول والدخول على الملوك وما يقصد منها فإن الحوائج تقضى واعمل للمحبات والقبول وجلب القلوب أوجد بها وتصلح لعمل الصناعات الحكمية والأكاسير الذهبية وتدبيرها (المنزلة الثالثة) منزلة الثريا ولها حرف الجيم إذا نزل القمر بها ينحدر منها روحانية ممتزجة الحرارة والرطوبة والبرودة وسعد متوسط جيد السفر ومازجة الأشراف والدخول على الأكابر وأرباب الدنيا وأهل القلم لأن الثريا متجمعة بكثرة من النجوم

ولذلك

**English:**
— 12 —

From the upper and the lower, and the servants of the letter Alif all together, except what you have heard and obeyed and stirred up, so-and-so and so-and-so, and by the right of what I have sworn to you by, and by the right of the letter Alif and what God has sent down in it of the secrets which no one becomes aware of except those who know God, تعالى, and by the right of Abjad and what is in it of the properties, except what you answered with obedience as I called you to it, and by what I swore to you by, and this is its form as you see, so understand, you will be guided.

And measure on this what is suitable to it of the works and the acts; your work will succeed, and God is the one who grants success to what is right.
And if you want for works of corruption, then do as we mentioned first: of hatred, destruction, ruin, and what resembles that, and measure upon it and do [it], you will attain the intended. And God says the truth, and He guides the way.

And this is the invocation of the letter Alif, with which you invoke the preceding number: Glory be to You, there is no god except You, Lord of everything and its inheritor, and the earth.

(The second station) the station of al-Batīn, and it is for the letter Bā’; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it, by the command of God تعالى, a spiritual force suitable for anger, and what was mentioned before in it: the medicine is drunk, and in it the great ones, and the sons of the world, and the kings of the earth move, because it is the second face of Aries, and it is the face of the sun, and in it is its nobility in nineteen degrees of it on the fourth day of April, and the sun is fortunate, except that it is hot, dry; so its fortune and its nobility in this face: in it you work for acceptance and entry upon kings and what is intended from it, for needs are fulfilled. And work for affections and acceptance and bringing of hearts; be found by it. And it is suitable for the work of الحكمي الصناعات and the golden elixirs and their management. (The third station) the station of الثريا, and it has the letter Jīm; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a spiritual force mixed with heat and moisture and coldness, and a medium fortune, good for travel, and mixed with the nobles and entry upon the great ones and the lords of the world and the people of the pen, because الثريا is gathered together in abundance from the stars.

And therefore

(المنزلة السادسة) منزلة الهنعة ولها حرف الواو وهي منزلة سعيدة تصلح للألفة والاجتماع بين المتباعدين لأنها

السابعة) منزلة الذراع ولها حرف الزاي إذا نزل القمر بها ينزل منها روحانية صالحة تعين الأمراض في

(المنزلة الرابعة عشر) منزلة السماك ولها حرف النون إذا نزل القمر بها ينزل منها روحانية لا تعين على
خير فلا تفعل فيها شيئاً ألبتة (المنزلة الخامسة عشر) منزلة الغفر ولها حرف السين إذا نزل القمر بها

لا يتحرك فيها إلا الخير (المنزلة السابعة عشر) منزلة الإكليل ولها حرف الفاء إذا نزل القمر بها ينزل

**English:**
- 13 -

And therefore the conjunction, with what we mentioned, was good, and for it
a great Wafq, worked in its nobility and carried, and by it
Abu Ja‘far al-Barmaki was acceptable with al-Rashid and obtained
from him what he desired; for its bearer, if he enters with it upon kings
and the great ones, obtains his desire from them, and no one مخالفة him
in what he desires. And this is its form, so understand, you will be guided, and God knows best.
(The fourth mansion) the mansion of al-Dabaran, and for it
the letter dal; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it
an evil Ruhaniyyah, working in it what is fitting for it of
harmful works and فساد, and what is fitting for that.
(The fifth mansion) the mansion of al-Haq‘ah, and for it the letter
ha; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah
mixed with the moderate hot, working in it works
of good and some of them of its opposite.

(The sixth mansion) the mansion of al-Han‘ah, and for it the letter waw, and it is a fortunate mansion, suitable for affection and conjunction between those far apart, because
there descends with it a good Ruhaniyyah, assisting illnesses upon treatments, assisting upon works of piety, righteousness, and success. (The mansion
seventh) the mansion of al-Dhira‘, and for it the letter zay; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a good Ruhaniyyah, assisting illnesses in
treatments, and perhaps whoever persists in its remembrance, there is opened for him something of the malakut. And it is good for retreats and seeking
the truth, and it is suitable for all works. (The eighth mansion) the mansion of al-Nathrah, and for it the letter ha; if the moon descends in it, there is manifested
from it a Ruhaniyyah not assisting toward good, working in it matters of فساد. (The ninth mansion) the mansion of al-Tarfah, and for it the letter
ta; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah like the preceding. (The tenth mansion) the mansion of al-Jabhah, and for it
the letter ya; if the moon descends, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah mixed between good and evil; do in it what is fitting for it. (The eleventh
mansion) the mansion of al-Zubrah, and for it the letter kaf; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah suitable for the growth of provisions
and the seeking of needs; do in it what is fitting for it. (The twelfth mansion) al-Sarfah, and for it the letter lam; if the moon descends
in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah mixed for good and evil; do in it what is fitting for it. (The thirteenth) the mansion of al-‘Awa’
and for it the letter mim; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah in which nothing moves except for riding the sea, no other
than that.
(The fourteenth mansion) the mansion of al-Samak, and for it the letter nun; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a Ruhaniyyah that does not assist toward
good, so do not do in it anything at all. (The fifteenth mansion) the mansion of al-Ghafr, and for it the letter sin; if the moon descends in it,
there descends from it a good Ruhaniyyah assisting upon all worldly and otherworldly movements; so do in it whatever you wish, your
work will succeed. (The sixteenth mansion) the mansion of al-Zubana, and for it the letter ‘ayn; if the moon descends in it, there descends from it a mixed Ruhaniyyah;
nothing moves in it except good. (The seventeenth mansion) the mansion of al-Iklil, and for it the letter fa; if the moon descends in it, there descends
